# kuberay: Agent Context

Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

Related pages: [Overview](index.md), [Extracted Data](details.md), [Security](security.md), [Network](network.md), [Dataflow](dataflow.md).

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

kuberay is a Kubernetes component. deploying 4 workload(s) (kuberay-operator, kuberay-operator, kuberay-operator, kuberay-operator) with 4 container(s). watching 12 resource type(s). exposing 2 service(s) on ports\: 443, 8080. Dockerfile EXPOSE ports\: 3000. with 6 webhook(s) (4 validating, 2 mutating). connecting to external services\: azure-blob, gcs, grpc, requests, urllib. runtime dependencies\: MinIO/S3, Redis, S3, kuberay-apiserver-service. with 993 operator config constants (20 image references, 150 env vars). exposing 8 Prometheus metrics. Python k8s client with 30 API call(s) and 1 CRD kind reference(s). integrates with Prometheus, management relationship via labels/annotations. deploys kube-rbac-proxy sidecar for RBAC-based authorization. references components\: vllm (import).
